# utils

## assertAdapterMethods

Synchronous adapter method check.

**Throws**

JobsCapabilityError when a method is missing.

**Example**

```ts
assertAdapterMethods(adapter);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const assertAdapterMethods: (adapter: JobsAdapterRuntime) => void;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/adapter-methods.ts#L51)

Checks the methods promised by a jobs adapter.

```ts
Effect.runSync(assertAdapterMethodsEffect(adapter));
```

# utils

## JobsAdapterRuntime (interface)

Provider protocol for submitting, reading, controlling, and observing jobs.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsAdapterRuntime {
  readonly kind: "jobs-adapter-runtime";
  readonly protocolVersion: typeof JOBS_PROTOCOL_VERSION;
  readonly capabilities: JobsCapabilityReport;
  readonly submit: (request: NativeSubmission, context: OperationContext) => Promise<NativeReceipt>;
  readonly get: (locator: NativeLocator, context: OperationContext) => Promise<NativeRun>;
  readonly list: (query: NativeRunQuery, context: OperationContext) => Promise<NativeRunPage>;
  readonly observe: (
    request: NativeWatchRequest,
    context: OperationContext,
  ) => AsyncIterable<NativeObservation>;
  readonly cancel: (
    request: NativeCancelRequest,
    context: OperationContext,
  ) => Promise<NativeControlReceipt>;
  readonly retry?: (
    request: NativeRetryRequest,
    context: OperationContext,
  ) => Promise<NativeControlReceipt>;
  readonly close: () => Promise<void>;
  readonly worker?: NativeTaskWorker;
  readonly schedules?: NativeScheduleOperations;
  readonly streams?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/adapter.types.ts#L93)

## NativeTaskWorker (interface)

Provider worker operations for accepting and completing task work.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface NativeTaskWorker {
  readonly next: (context: OperationContext) => Promise<NativeTaskWork | undefined>;
  readonly complete: (runId: string, output: unknown, context: OperationContext) => Promise<void>;
  readonly fail: (runId: string, error: unknown, context: OperationContext) => Promise<void>;
  /** Returns an intentional provider park/yield to the native continuation owner. */
  readonly suspend?: (runId: string, value: unknown, context: OperationContext) => Promise<void>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/adapter.types.ts#L123)

## TaskExecutionBinding (interface)

Provider-owned context and sinks supplied to one task execution.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskExecutionBinding {
  readonly run: {
    readonly runId: string;
    readonly jobId: string;
    readonly taskId: string;
    readonly taskVersion: string;
    readonly buildId: string;
    readonly service?: string;
    readonly serviceGeneration?: string;
    readonly attempt?: number;
    readonly acceptedAt?: string;
    readonly scheduledFor?: string;
    readonly parentRunId?: string;
    readonly deadlineMs?: number;
    readonly scope?: string;
    readonly inputSchemaHash?: string;
    readonly acceptanceIdentity?: string;
    readonly occurrenceIdentity?: string;
    readonly propagation?: TracePropagation;
  };
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly isSuspension?: (cause: unknown) => boolean;
  readonly sleep?: NativeDurableSleep;
  readonly progress?: NativeProgressWriter;
  readonly streams?: NativeStreamWriters;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/adapter.types.ts#L131)

# utils

## createCursorValue

Encodes a bounded authorization cursor, signing it when keys are supplied.

**Throws**

JobCursorError for invalid binding, key, or encoded size.

**Example**

```ts
createCursorValue(binding, { key: "secret" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const createCursorValue: (binding: JobCursorBinding, options?: JobCursorOptions) => string;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/authorization-cursor-codec.ts#L13)

## readCursorValue

Decodes and verifies a cursor against the caller's trusted authorization binding.

**Throws**

JobCursorError for invalid encoding, signature, or binding mismatch.

**Example**

```ts
readCursorValue(cursor, expected, { key: "secret" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const readCursorValue: (
  cursor: string,
  expected: JobCursorBinding,
  options?: JobCursorOptions,
) => JobCursorBinding;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/authorization-cursor-codec.ts#L47)

# utils

## JobClientAbortFailure (class)

Cancelled, timed out, or failed client enqueue, with the original cause.

**Example**

```ts
if (error instanceof JobClientAbortFailure) console.log(error.message);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class JobClientAbortFailure
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client-abort.ts#L8)

## runAbortable

Promise compatibility adapter for abortable client work.

**Throws**

The original cancellation, timeout, or provider error.

**Example**

```ts
await runAbortable(signal, undefined, () => Promise.resolve("ok"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runAbortable: <A>(
  signal: AbortSignal,
  deadlineMs: number | undefined,
  work: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<A>,
) => Promise<A>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client-abort.ts#L77)

## runAbortableEffect

Runs client work with an Effect owned abort listener and deadline.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(runAbortableEffect(signal, undefined, () => Promise.resolve("ok")));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runAbortableEffect: <A>(
  signal: AbortSignal,
  deadlineMs: number | undefined,
  work: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<A>,
) => Effect.Effect<Awaited<A>, JobClientAbortFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client-abort.ts#L19)

# utils

## EffectJobClient (interface)

A client whose enqueue operation is directly available in Effect.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface EffectJobClient<Input = unknown> extends JobClient<Input> {
  readonly enqueueEffect: (
    input: Input,
    options?: JobEnqueueOptions,
  ) => Effect.Effect<JobEnqueueResult, JobClientFailure>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L84)

## JobClient (interface)

Client operations exposed for a job through its provider.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobClient<Input = unknown> {
  readonly enqueue: (input: Input, options?: JobEnqueueOptions) => Promise<JobEnqueueResult>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L80)

## JobClientOptions (interface)

Options accepted for job client operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobClientOptions {
  readonly ownerId: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly source: unknown;
  readonly inputSchema?: StandardSchemaV1;
  readonly profile?: string;
  readonly resolveProfile?: (profile: string) => unknown;
  readonly bridge?: JobInvocationBridge;
  readonly signal?: () => AbortSignal;
  readonly deadline?: () => number | undefined;
  readonly correlationId?: string | (() => string | undefined);
  readonly declared?: boolean;
  readonly onDeclaredEdge?: (edge: JobDeclaredEdge) => void;
  readonly onObservedEdge?: (edge: JobObservedEdge) => void;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L64)

## JobClientState (interface)

Resolved dependencies shared by a client's enqueue operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobClientState {
  readonly options: JobClientOptions;
  readonly ownerId: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly profile: string;
  readonly declared: boolean;
  readonly provider: JobProvider;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L91)

## JobDeclaredEdge (interface)

Declared dependency between a task and job.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobDeclaredEdge {
  readonly kind: "enqueues-job";
  readonly from: string;
  readonly to: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L52)

## JobInvocationBridge (interface)

Bridge for invoking jobs from a task execution context.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobInvocationBridge {
  readonly run: <A>(
    operation: () => MaybePromise<A>,
    options?: JobInvocationBridgeOptions,
  ) => Promise<A>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L45)

## JobInvocationBridgeOptions (interface)

Options accepted for job invocation bridge operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobInvocationBridgeOptions {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly attributes: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly kind?: "producer";
  readonly input?: unknown;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L37)

## JobObservedEdge (interface)

Job edge recorded during a concrete invocation.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobObservedEdge {
  readonly relationship: "enqueues-job";
  readonly from: string;
  readonly to: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L58)

## JobOperationContext (interface)

Trusted job, scope, and signal data available during a client operation.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobOperationContext {
  readonly operation: "enqueue";
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly profile: string;
  readonly deadlineMs?: number;
  readonly correlationId?: string;
  readonly propagation?: TracePropagation;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L7)

## JobProvider (interface)

Provider callback used to resolve the jobs runtime.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobProvider {
  readonly enqueue: (
    input: unknown,
    options: JobEnqueueOptions,
    context: JobOperationContext,
  ) => MaybePromise<JobProviderResult>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L16)

## JobProviderResult (type alias)

Runtime or handle returned by the job provider.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobProviderResult = Pick<JobEnqueueResult, "instanceId" | "accepted"> &
  Partial<
    Pick<
      JobEnqueueResult,
      | "status"
      | "profile"
      | "correlationId"
      | "duplicate"
      | "idempotencyKey"
      | "idempotencyExpiresAt"
    >
  >;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/client.types.ts#L24)

# utils

## JobControlUnknownError (class)

A native control write may have committed; callers can recover by operation ID.

**Example**

```ts
new JobControlUnknownError("cancel-1", "same-key");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class JobControlUnknownError { constructor(
    readonly operationId: string,
    readonly idempotencyKey?: string,
    recovery: JobUnknownOutcome["recovery"] = { action: "inspect-native" },
  ) }
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L6)

### toJSON (method)

Serializes the bounded recovery contract for callers.

**Example**

```ts
error.toJSON();
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const toJSON: () => JobUnknownOutcome;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L31)

### code (property)

**Signature**

```ts

```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L7)

### outcome (property)

**Signature**

```ts

```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L8)

### recovery (property)

**Signature**


[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L9)

## JobObservationTimeoutError (class)

A native observation read exceeded its bounded timeout.


[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L75)

**Signature**

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-errors.ts#L76)

## JobResultUnavailableError (class)

A completed run has no result available to this caller.

**Signature**

Task execution cannot synchronously wait for another task result.

# utils

## JobObserveFailure (class)

Expected run observation failure with its original cause.

**Example**

```ts
if (error instanceof JobObserveFailure) console.log(error.message);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class JobObserveFailure
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-observe.ts#L8)

## observeWithTimeout

Compatibility async iterable with scoped iterator cleanup.

**Throws**

Original read, cancellation, or timeout error while consuming.

**Example**

```ts
for await (const frame of observeWithTimeout(source, signal, 1000)) console.log(frame);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const observeWithTimeout: (
  source: AsyncIterable<RunWatchFrame<RunSnapshot>>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  timeoutMs: number,
) => AsyncIterable<RunWatchFrame<RunSnapshot>>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-observe.ts#L31)

## observeWithTimeoutEffect

Creates a scoped run observation stream in Effect.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(
  Effect.flatMap(observeWithTimeoutEffect(source, signal, 1000), Stream.runCollect),
);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const observeWithTimeoutEffect: (
  source: AsyncIterable<RunWatchFrame<RunSnapshot>>,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  timeoutMs: number,
) => Effect.Effect<
  Stream.Stream<RunWatchFrame<RunSnapshot>, JobObserveFailure, never>,
  never,
  never
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-observe.ts#L19)

# utils

## JobControlWriteFailure (class)

A cancelled, uncertain, or provider-failed native control write.

**Example**

```ts
new JobControlWriteFailure({ operationId: "op", kind: "unknown", cause: new Error() });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class JobControlWriteFailure
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-write.ts#L9)

## controlWrite

Promise compatibility adapter for native control writes.

**Throws**

The original cancellation, unknown-outcome, or provider error.

**Example**

```ts
await controlWrite(() => Promise.resolve("ok"), undefined, "op");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const controlWrite: <A>(
  call: () => Promise<A>,
  signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  operationId: string,
) => Promise<A>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-write.ts#L72)

## controlWriteEffect

Calls a native control write with an Effect-managed abort listener.
Cancellation before the provider begins prevents the call. Cancellation
after start produces an unknown outcome requiring operation-id recovery.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(controlWriteEffect(() => Promise.resolve("ok"), undefined, "op"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const controlWriteEffect: <A>(
  call: () => Promise<A>,
  signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
  operationId: string,
) => Effect.Effect<A, JobControlWriteFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/control-write.ts#L23)

---

# Jobs

## defineJobValue

Binds a task to a named job with immutable scheduling and client policy metadata.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineJob } from "@relkit/app/jobs";
import { defineTask } from "@relkit/app/tasks";
import { z } from "@relkit/app/schema";
const task = defineTask({
  id: "receipts.send",
  version: "1",
  input: z.object({ orderId: z.string() }),
  output: z.object({ sent: z.boolean() }),
  handler: async () => ({ sent: true }),
});
const job = defineJob({ name: "sendReceipt", task });
void job;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineJobValue: <
  const Name extends string,
  const Task extends TaskDescriptorAny,
  const Id extends string = Name,
>(
  options: DefineJobOptions<Name, Task, Id>,
) => JobDescriptor<Name, Id, Task>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/define-job-value.ts#L64)

Since v0.4.1

# Jobs

## defineJob

Binds a task to an immutable job descriptor.

**Throws**

Original validation error for invalid definitions.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineJob, defineTask } from "@relkit/jobs";
import { z } from "@relkit/schema";
const echo = defineTask({
  id: "echo",
  version: "1",
  input: z.string(),
  output: z.string(),
  execution: "retryable",
  handler: async (input) => input,
});
const echoJob = defineJob({ name: "echoJob", task: echo });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineJob: <
  const Name extends string,
  const Task extends TaskDescriptorAny,
  const Id extends string = Name,
>(
  options: DefineJobOptions<Name, Task, Id>,
) => JobDescriptor<Name, Id, Task>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/define-job.ts#L49)

Since v0.4.1

# Jobs

## defineTaskValue

Declares a typed task with an immutable execution contract and handler.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineTask } from "@relkit/app/tasks";
import { z } from "@relkit/app/schema";
const exportOrders = defineTask({
  id: "orders.export",
  version: "1",
  input: z.object({ orderIds: z.array(z.string()) }),
  output: z.object({ exported: z.number() }),
  handler: async ({ orderIds }) => ({ exported: orderIds.length }),
});
void exportOrders;
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineTaskValue: <
  const Id extends string,
  const Version extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  const Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[] = readonly [],
  const Publishes extends readonly PublishedEventName[] = readonly [],
  const ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  const Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas = {},
  const Execution extends TaskExecution = "durable",
>(
  options: DefineTaskOptions<
    Id,
    Version,
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    Publishes,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Execution
  >,
) => TaskDescriptor<
  Id,
  Version,
  InputSchema,
  OutputSchema,
  Dependencies,
  Errors,
  Publishes,
  ProgressSchema,
  Streams,
  Execution
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/define-task-value.ts#L52)

Since v0.4.1

# utils

# Jobs

## defineTask

Declares a typed immutable task with an execution contract and handler.

**Throws**

Original validation error for invalid definitions.

**Example**

```ts
import { defineTask } from "@relkit/jobs";
import { z } from "@relkit/schema";
const echo = defineTask({
  id: "echo",
  version: "1",
  input: z.string(),
  output: z.string(),
  execution: "retryable",
  handler: async (input) => input,
});
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineTask: <
  const Id extends string,
  const Version extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  const Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[] = readonly [],
  const Publishes extends readonly PublishedEventName[] = readonly [],
  const ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  const Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas = {},
  const Execution extends TaskExecution = "durable",
>(
  options: DefineTaskOptions<
    Id,
    Version,
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    Publishes,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Execution
  >,
) => TaskDescriptor<
  Id,
  Version,
  InputSchema,
  OutputSchema,
  Dependencies,
  Errors,
  Publishes,
  ProgressSchema,
  Streams,
  Execution
>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/define-task.ts#L77)

Since v0.4.1

```ts
declare const defineTaskEffect: <
  const Id extends string,

  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  const Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[] = readonly [],
  const Publishes extends readonly PublishedEventName[] = readonly [],
  const ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  const Streams extends Readonly<Record<string, StandardSchemaV1<unknown, unknown>>> = {},
  const Execution extends TaskExecution = "durable",
>(
  options: DefineTaskOptions<
    Id,
    Version,
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    Publishes,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Execution
  >,
) => Effect.Effect<
  TaskDescriptor<
    Id,
    Version,
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    Publishes,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Execution

>;
```

# utils

## InstantValidationError (class)

An invalid authored RFC 3339 instant.

**Example**

```ts
new InstantValidationError({ name: "instant", reason: "invalid" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class InstantValidationError
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/instant-validation.ts#L11)

## assertRfc3339Instant

Synchronous RFC 3339 assertion.

**Throws**

TypeError when the instant is invalid.

**Example**

```ts
assertRfc3339Instant("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const assertRfc3339Instant: (value: unknown, name?: string) => asserts value is string;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/instant-validation.ts#L96)

## assertRfc3339InstantEffect

Requires an RFC 3339 instant in Effect.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(assertRfc3339InstantEffect("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const assertRfc3339InstantEffect: (
  value: unknown,
  name?: any,
) => Effect.Effect<undefined, InstantValidationError, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/instant-validation.ts#L76)

## isRfc3339Instant

Synchronous RFC 3339 predicate.

**Throws**

A runtime defect if the Effect cannot execute synchronously.

**Example**

```ts
isRfc3339Instant("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z");
```

**Signature**


# utils

## JOB_NAME_PATTERN

ASCII job names start with a lowercase letter and continue with letters or digits.

**Example**

```ts
JOB_NAME_PATTERN.test("sendEmail");
```

## JOB_NAME_RESERVED

JavaScript promise and object keys that cannot name callable jobs.

**Example**

```ts
JOB_NAME_RESERVED.includes("then");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const JOB_NAME_RESERVED: readonly [
  "then",
  "constructor",
  "prototype",
  "toJSON",
  "toString",
  "valueOf",
  "hasOwnProperty",
  "isPrototypeOf",
  "propertyIsEnumerable",
  "toLocaleString",
  "__proto__",
];
```

## `job-name.types.ts`

## Exports Grouped by Category

- [utils](#utils)
  - [JobName (type alias)](#jobname-type-alias)
  - [ValidJobName (type alias)](#validjobname-type-alias)

---

# utils

## JobName (type alias)

A validated job name, preserving literal types when available.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobName<Name extends string = string> = string extends Name ? string : ValidJobName<Name>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job-name.types.ts#L63)

## ValidJobName (type alias)

Compile-time predicate for a literal job name.

**Signature**

```ts
type ValidJobName<Name extends string = string> = string extends Name
  ? never
  : Name extends (typeof JOB_NAME_RESERVED)[number]
    ? never
    : Name extends `${LowercaseLetter}${infer Rest}`
      ? ValidCharacters<Rest> extends true
        ? AtMost64<Name> extends true
          ? Name
          : never
        : never
      : never;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job-name.types.ts#L50)

# utils

## copyAdmission

Validates and freezes job admission policy against the canonical input schema.

**Throws**

TypeError for unsupported or unsafe admission values.

**Example**

```ts
copyAdmission({ idempotency: { key: "orderId" } }, inputSchema);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyAdmission: <Input>(
  value: unknown,
  canonicalSchema?: StandardSchemaV1,
) => JobAdmission<Input> | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job-validation-value.ts#L25)

## copyClient

Validates the client operations and projected fields exposed by a job.

**Throws**

TypeError for an undeclared operation or invalid field.

**Example**

```ts
copyClient({ public: true, operations: ["status"] }, declarations);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyClient: (
  value: unknown,
  declarations: ClientDeclarations,
) => JobClientAccess | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job-validation-value.ts#L79)

## DefineJobOptions (type alias)

Options accepted for define job operations.

```ts
type DefineJobOptions<
  Name extends string,
  Task extends TaskDescriptorAny,
  Id extends string = Name,
> = DescriptorMetadata &
  JobServiceSelection & {
    readonly name: Name & ValidJobName<Name>;
    readonly id?: Id;
    readonly task: Task;
    readonly default?: boolean;
    readonly schedules?: readonly ScheduleDefinition<InferInput<Task["input"]>>[];
    readonly admission?: JobAdmission<InferOutput<Task["input"]>>;
    readonly client?: JobClientAccess<StreamsOf<Task>>;
  };
```

## JobAdmission (interface)

Policy for late schedules and idempotent submissions.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobAdmission<Input = unknown> {
  readonly pastAt?: "run" | "reject";
  readonly idempotency?: {
    readonly key?: Extract<keyof Input, string>;
    readonly retention?: DurationInput;
  };
}
```

## JobAuthorizationContext (interface)

Trusted scope and optional subject data passed to an access policy.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobAuthorizationContext {
  readonly application: string;
  readonly environment: string;
  readonly scope: string;
  readonly subject?: string;
  readonly auth?: unknown;
  readonly run?: import("@relkit/contracts/jobs").RunSnapshot;
}
```

## JobClientAccess (type alias)

Public or policy-guarded operations and fields exposed to clients.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobClientAccess<Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas = TaskStreamSchemas> =
  | {
      readonly public: true;
      readonly operations: readonly JobClientOperation[];
      readonly fields?: readonly JobClientField[];
      readonly streams?: readonly (keyof Streams & string)[];
    }
  | {
      readonly authorize: (
        request: JobAccessRequest,
        context: JobAuthorizationContext,
      ) => MaybePromise<JobAccessGrant>;
      readonly public?: never;
      readonly operations: readonly JobClientOperation[];
      readonly fields?: readonly JobClientField[];
      readonly streams?: readonly (keyof Streams & string)[];
    };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job.types.ts#L80)

Run fields that authorization may expose to a client.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobClientField = "status" | "input" | "progress" | "output" | "error";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job.types.ts#L69)

## JobDescriptor (type alias)

Validated job definition and its task-specific trigger operation.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobDescriptor<
  Name extends string,
  Id extends string,
  Task extends TaskDescriptorAny,
> = DescriptorBase<"job", Id> &
  JobRef<Id, Task["input"], Task["output"], Task> & {
    readonly name: Name;
    readonly task: Task;
    readonly input: Task["input"];
    readonly output: Task["output"];
    readonly errors?: Task["errors"];
    readonly progress?: Task["progress"];
    readonly streams?: Task["streams"];
    readonly execution: Task["execution"];
    readonly observation?: TaskObservation<
      Task["streams"] extends TaskStreamSchemas ? Task["streams"] : {}
    >;
    readonly service?: string;
    readonly default?: boolean;
    readonly schedules?: readonly ScheduleDefinition<InferOutput<Task["input"]>>[];
    readonly admission?: JobAdmission<InferOutput<Task["input"]>>;
    readonly client?: JobClientAccess<StreamsOf<Task>>;
    readonly trigger: (
      input: InferInput<Task["input"]>,
      options?: ServerTriggerOptions,
    ) => Promise<RunHandle>;
  };
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/job.types.ts#L126)

## JobsTelemetryService (interface)

Substitutable observer used by jobs Effect operations.

**Example**

```ts
const observer: JobsTelemetryService = { observe: (_name, effect) => effect };
```

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsTelemetryService {
  /** Surrounds an operation while preserving its success and error channels.
   * @param operation - A stable operation name.
   * @param effect - The operation to observe.
   * @returns The observed Effect with unchanged success and error types.
   * @example observer.observe("duration.toMillis", Effect.succeed(1000));
   */
  readonly observe: <A, E, R>(
    operation: JobsOperation,
    effect: Effect.Effect<A, E, R>,
  ) => Effect.Effect<A, E, R>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/jobs-observability.types.ts#L176)

## defineJob

Synchronous compatibility adapter for legacy job authoring.

**Throws**

TypeError when the authored job is invalid.

**Example**

```ts
defineJob(options);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const defineJob: <
  const Id extends string,
  const InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  const Target extends FunctionRefAny,
>(
  options: DefineJobOptions<Id, InputSchema, Target>,
) => JobDescriptor<Id, InferInput<InputSchema>, InputSchema, Target>;
```

## defineJobEffect

```ts
Effect.runSync(defineJobEffect(options));
```

```ts
declare const defineJobEffect: <
  const Id extends string,

  const Target extends FunctionRefAny,
>(
  options: DefineJobOptions<Id, InputSchema, Target>,
) => Effect.Effect<
  JobDescriptor<Id, InferInput<InputSchema>, InputSchema, Target>,
  LegacyJobValidationError,
  never
>;
```

## DefineJobOptions (interface)

Options accepted for define job operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DefineJobOptions<
  Id extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  Target extends FunctionRefAny,
> extends DescriptorMetadata {
  readonly id: Id;
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly target: Target;
  readonly profile?: string;
  readonly retry: RetryPolicy;
  readonly timeoutMs?: number;
  readonly concurrency?: number;
  readonly schedule?: readonly ScheduleDefinition<InferInput<InputSchema>>[];
  readonly idempotency?: IdempotencyDefinition<InferInput<InputSchema>>;
}
```

## JobDescriptor (interface)

Validated job definition and its task-specific trigger operation.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobDescriptor<
  Id extends string,
  Input,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 = StandardSchemaV1,
  Target extends FunctionRefAny = FunctionRefAny,
> extends DescriptorBase<"job", Id> {
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly target: Target;
  readonly profile?: string;
  readonly retry: RetryPolicy;
  readonly timeoutMs?: number;
  readonly concurrency?: number;
  readonly schedule?: readonly ScheduleDefinition<Input>[];
  readonly idempotency?: IdempotencyDefinition<Input>;
}
```

## breakingEffect

Constructs one breaking diagnostic within a migration Effect.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runSync(breakingEffect("TASK_ID_CHANGED", "Task changed"));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const breakingEffect: (
  code:
    | "TASK_ID_CHANGED"
    | "TASK_VERSION_CHANGED"
    | "TASK_INPUT_SCHEMA_CHANGED"
    | "DEDUPE_SCOPE_CHANGED"
    | "PUBLIC_CLIENT_FINGERPRINT_CHANGED"
    | "LEGACY_JOBS_DISABLED"
    | "LEGACY_ALIAS_CONFLICT",
  message: string,
) => Effect.Effect<JobMigrationDiagnostic, never, never>;
```

```ts
    | "LEGACY_JOBS_DISABLED"
    | "LEGACY_ALIAS_CONFLICT"

}
```

## JobBindingErrorCode (type alias)

Machine-readable reason for a binding resolution failure.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobBindingErrorCode =
  | "MISSING_IMPLICIT_NAME"
  | "INVALID_IMPLICIT_NAME"
  | "TASK_SELECTOR_MISMATCH"
  | "UNKNOWN_JOB_SELECTOR"
  | "AMBIGUOUS_JOB"
  | "DUPLICATE_JOB_ID"
  | "MULTIPLE_DEFAULT_JOBS"
  | "MISSING_JOB_PROFILE"
  | "AMBIGUOUS_JOB_PROFILE"
  | "UNKNOWN_JOB_PROFILE";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/resolve-binding.types.ts#L33)

## ResolveBindingOptions (interface)

Inputs for resolving one task to its executable job.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ResolveBindingOptions {
  readonly task: TaskRefAny;
  readonly jobs?: readonly JobDescriptorAny[];
  readonly selector?: JobDescriptorAny | JobRefAny;
  readonly implicitName?: string;
  readonly profiles?: readonly string[] | Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
  readonly defaultProfile?: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/resolve-binding.types.ts#L8)

## ResolvedTaskBinding (interface)

Canonical task-to-job selection returned by the binding resolver.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ResolvedTaskBinding {
  readonly taskId: string;
  readonly taskVersion?: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly service?: string;
  readonly profile: string;
  readonly source: BindingSource;
  readonly implicit: boolean;
  readonly default: boolean;
  readonly private: boolean;
  readonly job?: JobDescriptorAny;
}
```

## RunLocatorPayload (interface)

**Signature**

```ts
export interface RunLocatorPayload {
  readonly application: string;
  readonly environment: string;
  readonly serviceGeneration: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly taskId: string;
  readonly taskVersion: string;
  readonly buildId: string;

  readonly namespaceHash?: string;
  readonly schemaHash?: string;
  readonly native: { readonly kind: string; readonly value: string };
}
```

# utils

## JobsManifestLike (interface)

Minimal manifest fields used to resolve authored jobs and tasks.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsManifestLike {
  readonly protocol?: string;
  readonly version?: number;
  readonly app?: string;
  readonly environment?: string;
  readonly jobsProtocolVersion?: number;
  readonly tasks?: readonly {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly version?: string;
    readonly buildId?: string;
    readonly schemaHashes?: unknown;
    readonly policy?: unknown;
  }[];
  readonly jobs?: readonly {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly name: string;
    readonly taskId: string;
    readonly taskVersion?: string;
    readonly buildId?: string;
    readonly profile?: string;
    readonly serviceGeneration?: string;
    readonly default?: boolean;
  }[];
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/runtime.types.ts#L12)

## JobsOperationOptions (type alias)

Caller signal and trusted metadata passed to a native jobs operation.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobsOperationOptions = Pick<OperationContext, "signal"> &
  Partial<
    Pick<
      OperationContext,
      | "scope"
      | "operationId"
      | "deadlineMs"
      | "correlationId"
      | "parentRunId"
      | "propagation"
      | "acceptanceIdentity"
      | "occurrenceIdentity"
      | "inputSchemaHash"
      | "retryOfRunId"
    >
  >;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/runtime.types.ts#L67)

## JobsRuntime (interface)

Validated runtime bindings and provider access for job operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsRuntime {
  readonly adapter: JobsAdapterRuntime;
  readonly capabilities: JobsCapabilityReport;
  readonly application: string;
  readonly environment: string;
  readonly scope: string;
  readonly service: string;
  readonly serviceGeneration: string;
  readonly manifest?: JobsManifestLike;
  readonly jobs?: readonly JobDescriptorAny[];
  readonly taskExecutor?: TaskExecutor;
  readonly tasks?: readonly TaskDescriptorAny[];
  readonly resolveBinding: (task: TaskRefAny, selector?: JobRefAny) => JobsRuntimeBinding;
  readonly operationContext: (options: JobsOperationOptions) => OperationContext;
  readonly close: () => Promise<void>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/runtime.types.ts#L84)

## JobsRuntimeBinding (interface)

Resolved task, job, service, and build identity pinned for one run.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsRuntimeBinding {
  readonly taskId: string;
  readonly taskVersion: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly profile: string;
  readonly service: string;
  readonly serviceGeneration: string;
  readonly buildId: string;
  readonly inputSchemaHash?: string;
  readonly scope?: string;
  readonly policy?: unknown;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/runtime.types.ts#L37)

## JobsRuntimeOptions (interface)

Provider, manifest, and authored descriptors used to create a jobs runtime.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobsRuntimeOptions {
  readonly adapter?: unknown;
```

TypeError for non-JSON schedule input.

```ts
nativeDefinition(schedule, options, "owner-1");
```

```ts
declare const nativeDefinition: (
  schedule: ScheduleReconciliationOptions["desired"][number],
  options: Omit<ScheduleReconciliationOptions, "native">,

) => Readonly<
  | {
      metadata: Readonly<{
        buildId: string;
        serviceGeneration: string;
        canonicalInput: string;
        taskVersion?: string;
        taskId?: string;
        owner: string;
        application: string;
        environment: string;
        service: string;
        scope: string;
        jobId: string;
      }>;
      id: string;
      input: unknown;
      overlap?: ScheduleOverlap;
      misfire?: ScheduleMisfire;
      cron: string;
      timezone: string;
      every?: never;
    }
  | {
      metadata: Readonly<{
        buildId: string;
        serviceGeneration: string;
        canonicalInput: string;
        taskVersion?: string;
        taskId?: string;
        owner: string;
        application: string;
        environment: string;
        service: string;
        scope: string;
        jobId: string;
      }>;
      id: string;
      input: unknown;
      overlap?: ScheduleOverlap;
      misfire?: ScheduleMisfire;
      every: DurationInput;
      cron?: never;
      timezone?: never;

>;
```

# utils

## JobScheduleClient (interface)

Read and write operations available for a job's schedules.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface JobScheduleClient<Definition = ScheduleDefinition> {
  readonly list: (options?: ScheduleListOptions) => Promise<ScheduleReadReceipt<Definition>>;
  readonly get: (
    id: string,
    options?: { readonly signal?: AbortSignal },
  ) => Promise<ScheduleReadReceipt<Definition>>;
  readonly upsert: (
    definition: Definition,
    options: ScheduleWriteOptions,
  ) => Promise<ScheduleWriteReceipt<Definition>>;
  readonly pause: (
    id: string,
    options: ScheduleWriteOptions,
  ) => Promise<ScheduleWriteReceipt<Definition>>;
  readonly resume: (
    id: string,
    options: ScheduleWriteOptions,
  ) => Promise<ScheduleWriteReceipt<Definition>>;
  readonly delete: (
    id: string,
    options: ScheduleWriteOptions,
  ) => Promise<ScheduleWriteReceipt<Definition>>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L67)

## ScheduleDefinition (type alias)

Authored schedule target, timing, and admission policy.

**Signature**

```ts
type ScheduleDefinition<Input = unknown> = {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly input: Input;
  readonly overlap?: ScheduleOverlap;
  readonly misfire?: ScheduleMisfire;
} & (
  | { readonly cron: string; readonly timezone: string; readonly every?: never }
  | { readonly every: DurationInput; readonly cron?: never; readonly timezone?: never }
);
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L10)

## ScheduleListOptions (interface)

Options accepted for schedule list operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ScheduleListOptions {
  readonly limit?: number;
  readonly cursor?: string;
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L44)

## ScheduleMisfire (type alias)

How missed occurrences are handled after downtime.

**Signature**

```ts
type ScheduleMisfire = "skip" | "latest" | "all";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L7)

## ScheduleOverlap (type alias)

Whether simultaneous occurrences are skipped or allowed.

**Signature**

```ts
type ScheduleOverlap = "allow" | "skip";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L5)

## ScheduleReadReceipt (interface)

Receipt returned after a schedule read operation.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ScheduleReadReceipt<Definition = ScheduleDefinition> {
  readonly outcome: "available" | "unavailable";
  readonly schedule?: ScheduleRecord<Definition>;
  readonly schedules?: readonly ScheduleRecord<Definition>[];
  readonly nextCursor?: string;
  readonly hasMore?: boolean;
  readonly reason?: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L30)

## ScheduleRecord (interface)

Provider read model for a persisted schedule.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ScheduleRecord<Definition = ScheduleDefinition> {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly jobId: string;
  readonly state: "active" | "paused" | "missing" | "unknown";
  readonly definition: Definition;
  readonly observedAt?: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L21)

## ScheduleWriteOptions (interface)

Options accepted for schedule write operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ScheduleWriteOptions {
  readonly operationId: string;
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L51)

## ScheduleWriteOutcome (type alias)

Result category for a native schedule mutation.

**Signature**

```ts
type ScheduleWriteOutcome =
  "created" | "updated" | "paused" | "resumed" | "deleted" | "requested" | "unsupported";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L40)

## ScheduleWriteReceipt (type alias)

Receipt returned after a schedule write operation.

**Signature**

```ts
type ScheduleWriteReceipt<Definition = ScheduleDefinition> =
  | {
      readonly operationId: string;
      readonly scheduleId: string;
      readonly outcome: ScheduleWriteOutcome;
      readonly schedule?: ScheduleRecord<Definition>;
    }
  | (JobUnknownOutcome & { readonly scheduleId: string });
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/schedule.types.ts#L57)

```ts
  readonly scope?: string;

}
```

## JobSubmissionUnknownError (class)

Native acceptance may have committed and needs idempotent recovery.

```ts
    readonly operationId: string,
    readonly idempotencyKey: string | undefined,
    recovery: JobUnknownOutcome["recovery"] = { action: "inspect-native" },
  ) }
```

# utils

## boundedKey

Checks the 256-byte idempotency key limit.

**Throws**

TypeError for oversized keys.

**Example**

```ts
boundedKey("order-1");
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const boundedKey: (value: string) => string;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L127)

## currentCorrelation

**Signature**

```ts
declare const currentCorrelation: () => string | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L16)

## currentTaskRunId

**Signature**

```ts
declare const currentTaskRunId: () => string | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L18)

## explicitOrDerivedKey

Resolves explicit or input-derived idempotency and checks agreement.

**Throws**

TypeError for mismatched or nonscalar keys.

**Example**

```ts
explicitOrDerivedKey(job, options, { orderId: "one" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const explicitOrDerivedKey: (
  job: { readonly admission?: { readonly idempotency?: { readonly key?: string } } } | undefined,
  options: CopiedTriggerOptions,
  input: JsonValue | undefined,
) => string | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L92)

## isRecord

Tests for a nonarray provider record.

**Example**

```ts
isRecord({ accepted: true });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const isRecord: (value: unknown) => value is Record<string, unknown>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L175)

## isUnknown

Recognizes a native unknown submission response with recovery data.

**Example**

```ts
isUnknown(response);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const isUnknown: (value: unknown) => value is UnknownSubmissionOutcome;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L159)

## normalizeReceipt

Validates that native acceptance matches the pinned task and job binding.

**Throws**

JobReceiptError or JobSubmissionUnknownError for invalid outcomes.

**Example**

```ts
normalizeReceipt(receipt, binding, metadata);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const normalizeReceipt: (
  value: NativeReceipt | unknown,
  binding: JobsRuntimeBinding,
  metadata: TaskSubmissionMetadata,
) => RunHandle;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L45)

## normalizeSubmissionError

Normalizes provider rejection and uncertain acceptance into public errors.

**Example**

```ts
normalizeSubmissionError(cause, metadata);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const normalizeSubmissionError: (cause: unknown, metadata: TaskSubmissionMetadata) => Error;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L26)

## propagationFor

**Signature**

```ts
declare const propagationFor: (correlationId: string | undefined) => TracePropagation | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L17)

## scheduledTime

Resolves a trigger's absolute time or delay against a supplied clock.

**Throws**

TypeError for an invalid duration.

**Example**

```ts
scheduledTime({ delay: "1 second" }, 0);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const scheduledTime: (options: CopiedTriggerOptions, now: number) => string | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L139)

## validatedEnvelope

Rebuilds a canonical wire envelope from admitted input.

**Example**

```ts
validatedEnvelope(admission);
```

```ts

```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-support-value.ts#L149)

# utils

## JobSubmissionWriteFailure (class)

Cancelled, uncertain, or provider failed native submission.

**Example**

```ts
if (error instanceof JobSubmissionWriteFailure) console.log(error.message);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare class JobSubmissionWriteFailure
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-write.ts#L11)

## submitAbortable

Promise compatibility adapter for native submission.

**Throws**

The original cancellation, unknown outcome, or provider error.

**Example**

```ts
await submitAbortable(adapter, request, context, signal, metadata);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const submitAbortable: (
  adapter: JobsAdapterRuntime,
  request: Parameters<JobsAdapterRuntime["submit"]>[0],
  context: OperationContext,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  metadata: TaskSubmissionMetadata,
) => Promise<unknown>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-write.ts#L84)

## submitAbortableEffect

Submits with an Effect owned abort listener and uncertain outcome semantics.

**Example**

```ts
Effect.runPromise(submitAbortableEffect(adapter, request, context, signal, metadata));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const submitAbortableEffect: (
  adapter: JobsAdapterRuntime,
  request: NativeSubmission,
  context: OperationContext,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  metadata: TaskSubmissionMetadata,
) => Effect.Effect<NativeReceipt, JobSubmissionWriteFailure, never>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/submission-write.ts#L24)

```ts
export interface CanonicalTaskSubmissionOptions {
  readonly canonicalInput: JobWireEnvelope;

  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
  readonly scheduledFor?: string;
  readonly occurrenceIdentity?: string;
  readonly acceptanceIdentity?: string;

  readonly inputHash?: string;
  readonly inputSchemaHash?: string;

}
```

```ts
export interface SubmissionAdmission {
  readonly task: TaskDescriptorAny;
  readonly job?: JobDescriptorAny;

  readonly input: JsonValue;
  readonly canonicalInput: JsonValue | undefined;
  readonly inputHash: string;
  readonly metadata: TaskSubmissionMetadata;
}
```

```ts
    options?: unknown,

}
```

```ts
  : unknown;
```

## TaskContext (type alias)

Execution context assembled from the task's declared dependencies and capabilities.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskContext<
  Execution extends TaskExecution = TaskExecution,
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas = {},
  Publishes extends readonly string[] = readonly [],
> = TaskContextBase<Dependencies, Publishes> &
  (Execution extends "durable"
    ? {
        readonly sleep: (duration: DurationInput, options: TaskSleepOptions) => Promise<void>;
        readonly sleepUntil: (instant: string, options: TaskSleepOptions) => Promise<void>;
      }
    : {}) &
  ([ProgressSchema] extends [StandardSchemaV1]
    ? { readonly progress: TaskProgressEmitter<InferInput<ProgressSchema>> }
    : {}) &
  ([keyof Streams] extends [never] ? {} : { readonly streams: TaskStreamEmitters<Streams> });
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-core.types.ts#L131)

## TaskContextBase (interface)

Common invocation context shared by task handlers and hooks.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskContextBase<
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  Publishes extends readonly string[] = readonly [],
> {
  readonly run: TaskRunContext;
  readonly invocation: InvocationMetadata;
  readonly signal: AbortSignal;
  readonly env: ResolvedApplicationEnv;
  readonly log: PublicLogger;
  readonly trace: PublicTrace;
  readonly time: PublicClock;
  readonly tasks: TaskClients<Dependencies["tasks"]>;
  readonly jobs: JobTriggerClients<Dependencies["jobs"]>;
  readonly agents: AgentClients<Dependencies["agents"]>;
  readonly buckets: BucketClients<Dependencies["buckets"]>;
  readonly cache: CacheClients<Dependencies["cache"]>;
  readonly events: EventClients<PublishedEventMap<Publishes>>;
  readonly database: RegisteredContext<"database", Readonly<Record<string, never>>>;
  readonly auth: RegisteredContext<"auth", TaskAuthContext>;
  readonly constants: RegisteredContext<"constants", Readonly<Record<string, never>>>;
  readonly prompts: RegisteredContext<"prompts", Readonly<Record<string, never>>>;
  readonly idempotencyKey: (operation: string) => string;
}
```

## TaskFailureHook (type alias)

Hook invoked after task execution fails.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskFailureHook<
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies,
  Publishes extends readonly string[] = readonly [],
> = (error: unknown, context: TaskHookContext<Dependencies, Publishes>) => Promise<void>;
```

## TaskHandler (type alias)

Handler receives validated input and scoped context, then returns output or a declared failure.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskHandler<
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  Execution extends TaskExecution,
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies,
  Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[],
  ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined,
  Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas,
  Publishes extends readonly string[],
> = (
  input: InferOutput<InputSchema>,
  context: TaskContext<Execution, Dependencies, ProgressSchema, Streams, Publishes>,
) => MaybePromise<TaskHandlerResult<InferInput<OutputSchema>, Errors>>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-definition-support.types.ts#L13)

Allowed output and declared failure forms returned by a task handler.

# utils

## DefineTaskOptions (interface)

Authoring contract for task schemas, dependencies, hooks, and execution policy.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface DefineTaskOptions<
  Id extends string,
  Version extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[] = readonly [],
  Publishes extends readonly PublishedEventName[] = readonly [],
  ProgressSchema extends StandardSchemaV1 | undefined = undefined,
  Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas = {},
  Execution extends TaskExecution = "durable",
> extends DescriptorMetadata {
  readonly id: Id;
  readonly version: Version;
  readonly input: InputSchema;
  readonly inputWire?: StandardSchemaV1<InferOutput<InputSchema>, InferOutput<InputSchema>>;
  readonly output: OutputSchema;
  readonly errors?: Errors;
  readonly execution?: Execution;
  readonly dependencies?: ValidTaskDependencies<Dependencies>;
  readonly publishes?: Publishes;
  readonly progress?: ProgressSchema;
  readonly streams?: Streams;
  readonly observation?: TaskObservation<Streams, ProgressSchema>;
  readonly retry?: TaskRetryPolicy;
  readonly resources?: TaskResources;
  readonly concurrency?: TaskConcurrency<CanonicalInputKey<InputSchema>>;
  readonly maxDuration?: DurationInput;
  readonly maxElapsed?: DurationInput;
  readonly logging?: TaskLogging;
  readonly onStart?: TaskStartHook<InputSchema, Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly onSuccess?: TaskSuccessHook<OutputSchema, Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly onFailure?: TaskFailureHook<Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly handler: TaskHandler<
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Execution,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Publishes
  >;
}
```

## TaskDescriptor (interface)

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskDescriptor<
  Id extends string,
  Version extends string,
  InputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  OutputSchema extends StandardSchemaV1,
  Dependencies extends TaskDependencies = {},
  Errors extends readonly ErrorDescriptorAny[] = readonly [],
  Publishes extends readonly string[] = readonly [],

>
  extends DescriptorBase<"task", Id>, TaskRef<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, Errors> {
  readonly version: Version;
  readonly execution: Execution;
  readonly inputWire?: StandardSchemaV1<InferOutput<InputSchema>, InferOutput<InputSchema>>;
  readonly dependencies?: Dependencies;
  readonly publishes?: Publishes;
  readonly progress?: ProgressSchema;
  readonly streams?: Streams;
  readonly observation?: TaskObservation<Streams, ProgressSchema>;
  readonly retry: NormalizedTaskRetryPolicy;
  readonly resources?: TaskResources;
  readonly concurrency?: TaskConcurrency<CanonicalInputKey<InputSchema>>;
  readonly maxDuration?: DurationInput;
  readonly maxElapsed?: DurationInput;
  readonly logging?: TaskLogging;
  readonly onStart?: TaskStartHook<InputSchema, Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly onSuccess?: TaskSuccessHook<OutputSchema, Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly onFailure?: TaskFailureHook<Dependencies, Publishes>;
  readonly handler: TaskHandler<
    InputSchema,
    OutputSchema,
    Execution,
    Dependencies,
    Errors,
    ProgressSchema,
    Streams,
    Publishes
  >;

    input: InferInput<InputSchema>,
    options?: TaskTriggerOptions<TaskRef<Id, InputSchema, OutputSchema, Errors>>,
  ) => Promise<RunHandle>;
}
```

## TaskStreamEmitters (type alias)

Named emitters whose input types follow each declared stream schema.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskStreamEmitters<Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas> = {
  readonly [Name in keyof Streams & string]: TaskStreamEmitter<TaskStreamInput<Streams[Name]>>;
};
```

## TaskStreamFrames (type alias)

Discriminated frames for all declared task streams.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskStreamFrames<Streams extends TaskStreamSchemas> = {
  readonly [Name in keyof Streams & string]: NamedStreamFrame<TaskStreamOutput<Streams[Name]>> & {
    readonly name: Name;
  };
}[keyof Streams & string];
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-emission.types.ts#L30)

## retryDelayMillis

Synchronous bounded retry delay calculator.

**Example**

```ts
retryDelayMillis(policy, 2, 0, () => 0.5);
```

**Signature**

```ts

  policy: NormalizedTaskRetryPolicy,
```

## retryDelayMillisEffect

Computes bounded retry delay in Effect using an injectable sample source.

```ts
  ),
);
```

```ts
  policy: NormalizedTaskRetryPolicy,
  attempt: number,
  retryAfterMillis?: any,
  random?: (() => number) | undefined,
) => Effect.Effect<number, TaskValidationFailure, never>;
```

TypeError for invalid levels or redaction names.

**Example**

```ts
copyLogging({ level: "info", redact: ["secret"] });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyLogging: (value: unknown) => TaskLogging | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-policy-value-copy.ts#L113)

## copyResources

Validates CPU and exact memory for a task resource request.

**Throws**

TypeError for invalid CPU or memory.

**Example**

```ts
copyResources({ cpu: 1, memory: "1 GiB" });
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyResources: (value: unknown) => TaskResources | undefined;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-policy-value-copy.ts#L34)

## copyTaskTags

Copies unique, bounded task tags into a frozen list.

**Throws**

TypeError for duplicates, invalid text, or excess tags.

**Example**

```ts
copyTaskTags(["urgent"]);
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const copyTaskTags: (value: unknown) => readonly string[] | undefined;
```

# utils

## EffectTaskEmitter (interface)

An emitter with a typed Effect operation alongside its Promise adapter.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface EffectTaskEmitter<Value> {
  readonly emit: (value: Value) => Promise<ProgressEmitReceipt>;
  readonly emitEffect: (value: Value) => Effect.Effect<ProgressEmitReceipt, TaskEmissionFailure>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-progress.types.ts#L42)

## TaskEmissionErrorCode (type alias)

Stable compatibility error codes for task emissions.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskEmissionErrorCode =
  | "RELKIT_TASK_PROGRESS_INVALID"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_STREAM_INVALID"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_PROGRESS_TOO_LARGE"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_STREAM_TOO_LARGE"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_PROGRESS_PERSISTENCE"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_STREAM_PERSISTENCE"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_PROGRESS_RETENTION"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_STREAM_RETENTION"
  | "RELKIT_TASK_EMISSION_ABORTED";
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-progress.types.ts#L31)

## TaskEmissionSink (type alias)

Sink for a validated progress item.

**Signature**

```ts
type TaskEmissionSink = (
  value: unknown,
  signal: AbortSignal,
) => MaybePromise<ProgressEmitReceipt | void>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-progress.types.ts#L6)

## TaskEmitterOptions (interface)

Delivery and retention settings for a progress emitter.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskEmitterOptions {
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
  readonly sink?: TaskEmissionSink;
  readonly durable?: boolean;
  readonly maxBytes?: number;
  readonly generation?: string;
  readonly requireGeneration?: boolean;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-progress.types.ts#L11)

## TaskStreamEmitterOptions (interface)

Delivery settings for a named stream emitter.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskStreamEmitterOptions extends Omit<TaskEmitterOptions, "sink"> {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly sink?: TaskStreamSink;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-progress.types.ts#L20)

## TaskStreamSink (type alias)

Sink for a validated named stream item.

**Signature**

```ts

  value: unknown,

) => MaybePromise<ProgressEmitReceipt | void>;
```

## TaskWireFailure (class)


## runWirePromise

Runs an Effect wire operation for Promise compatibility.

**Throws**

The original wire validation error on failure.

**Example**

```ts
await runWirePromise(wirePromiseEffect("wire.decode", async () => decoded));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runWirePromise: <A>(effect: Effect.Effect<A, TaskWireFailure>) => Promise<A>;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-wire-run.ts#L67)

## runWireSync

Runs an Effect wire operation for synchronous compatibility.

**Throws**

The original wire validation error on failure.

**Example**

```ts
runWireSync(wireEffect("wire.encode", () => encoded));
```

**Signature**

```ts
declare const runWireSync: <A>(effect: Effect.Effect<A, TaskWireFailure>) => A;
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/task-wire-run.ts#L56)

## wireEffect

Lifts a synchronous wire operation into observed Effect.

**Example**

```ts
wireEffect("wire.encode", () => encoded);
```

**Signature**

```ts
  operation: JobsOperation,

```

```ts
wirePromiseEffect("wire.decode", async () => decoded);
```

```ts

) => Effect.Effect<A, TaskWireFailure, never>;
```

TaskWireValidationError or TypeError for invalid data or limit.

## JobDescriptorForThisTask (type alias)

Job reference constrained to target the selected task.

**Signature**

```ts
type JobDescriptorForThisTask<Task extends TaskRefAny = TaskRefAny> = JobRefAny & {
  readonly task: Task;
};
```

## RunResultOptions (interface)

Options accepted for run result operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface RunResultOptions {
  readonly timeout: DurationInput;
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/trigger.types.ts#L32)

## ServerTriggerOptions (interface)

Options accepted for server trigger operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface ServerTriggerOptions extends TriggerOptions {
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/trigger.types.ts#L15)

## TaskTriggerOptions (interface)

Options accepted for task trigger operations.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TaskTriggerOptions<
  Task extends TaskRefAny = TaskRefAny,
> extends ServerTriggerOptions {
  readonly job?: JobDescriptorForThisTask<Task>;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/trigger.types.ts#L25)

## TriggerOptions (interface)

Browser-safe submission fields. Server-only signals and selectors are separate.

**Signature**

```ts
export interface TriggerOptions {
  readonly operationId?: string;
  readonly idempotencyKey?: string;
  readonly delay?: DurationInput;
  readonly at?: string;
  readonly tags?: readonly string[];
  readonly correlationId?: string;
}
```

[Source](https://github.com/rel-kit/relkit/blob/main/packages/jobs/src/trigger.types.ts#L5)
